\index{page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}!alloc\_pages\_exact@{alloc\_pages\_exact}}
\index{alloc\_pages\_exact@{alloc\_pages\_exact}!page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_pages\_exact()}{alloc\_pages\_exact()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void$\ast$ alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_aad0f8411dfc957b4069125f330192f4f}{size\+\_\+t}}}]{size,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_ac7af3d394e1fddbf83385f928a7e41ab}{gfp\+\_\+t}}}]{gfp\+\_\+mask }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact -\/ allocate an exact number physically-\/contiguous pages. @size\+: the number of bytes to allocate @gfp\+\_\+mask\+: G\+FP flags for the allocation, must not contain \+\_\+\+\_\+\+G\+F\+P\+\_\+\+C\+O\+MP

This function is similar to \mbox{\hyperlink{mempolicy_8c_add7a36e6a0c552051c3453df754b9c7b}{alloc\+\_\+pages()}}, except that it allocates the minimum number of pages to satisfy the request. \mbox{\hyperlink{mempolicy_8c_add7a36e6a0c552051c3453df754b9c7b}{alloc\+\_\+pages()}} can only allocate memory in power-\/of-\/two pages.

This function is also limited by M\+A\+X\+\_\+\+O\+R\+D\+ER.

Memory allocated by this function must be released by \mbox{\hyperlink{page__alloc_8c_aa0585d3acdf6b0d001ccaf5847a377a7}{free\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact()}}.

Return\+: pointer to the allocated area or N\+U\+LL in case of error. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_pages\_exact\_nid()}{alloc\_pages\_exact\_nid()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void$\ast$ \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2init_8h_a4c59245c0fee67ba373330c8fd415acb}{\+\_\+\+\_\+meminit}} alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact\+\_\+nid (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}}}]{nid,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_aad0f8411dfc957b4069125f330192f4f}{size\+\_\+t}}}]{size,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_ac7af3d394e1fddbf83385f928a7e41ab}{gfp\+\_\+t}}}]{gfp\+\_\+mask }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact\+\_\+nid -\/ allocate an exact number of physically-\/contiguous pages on a node. @nid\+: the preferred node ID where memory should be allocated @size\+: the number of bytes to allocate @gfp\+\_\+mask\+: G\+FP flags for the allocation, must not contain \+\_\+\+\_\+\+G\+F\+P\+\_\+\+C\+O\+MP

Like \mbox{\hyperlink{page__alloc_8c_ac456983d3e3b43bc0a2a9c5615622211}{alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact()}}, but try to allocate on node nid first before falling back.

\index{page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}!free\_area\_init@{free\_area\_init}}
\index{free\_area\_init@{free\_area\_init}!page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{free\_area\_init()}{free\_area\_init()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \mbox{\hyperlink{test__stackinit_8c_aca8f1e05ca62886e6d99e8dc006e5500}{\+\_\+\+\_\+init}} free\+\_\+area\+\_\+init (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned long $\ast$}]{max\+\_\+zone\+\_\+pfn }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

free\+\_\+area\+\_\+init -\/ Initialise all pg\+\_\+data\+\_\+t and zone data @max\+\_\+zone\+\_\+pfn\+: an array of max P\+F\+Ns for each zone

This will call \mbox{\hyperlink{page__alloc_8c_a6b886554b9410f823b7af999e84006fc}{free\+\_\+area\+\_\+init\+\_\+node()}} for each active node in the system. Using the page ranges provided by \mbox{\hyperlink{memblock_8c_ad0e685a82a3772b25b78e30e34fa8595}{memblock\+\_\+set\+\_\+node()}}, the size of each zone in each node and their holes is calculated. If the maximum P\+FN between two adjacent zones match, it is assumed that the zone is empty. For example, if arch\+\_\+max\+\_\+dma\+\_\+pfn == arch\+\_\+max\+\_\+dma32\+\_\+pfn, it is assumed that arch\+\_\+max\+\_\+dma32\+\_\+pfn has no pages. It is also assumed that a zone starts where the previous one ended. For example, Z\+O\+N\+E\+\_\+\+D\+M\+A32 starts at arch\+\_\+max\+\_\+dma\+\_\+pfn. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{get\_pfn\_range\_for\_nid()}{get\_pfn\_range\_for\_nid()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \mbox{\hyperlink{test__stackinit_8c_aca8f1e05ca62886e6d99e8dc006e5500}{\+\_\+\+\_\+init}} get\+\_\+pfn\+\_\+range\+\_\+for\+\_\+nid (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}}}]{nid,  }\item[{unsigned long $\ast$}]{start\+\_\+pfn,  }\item[{unsigned long $\ast$}]{end\+\_\+pfn }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

get\+\_\+pfn\+\_\+range\+\_\+for\+\_\+nid -\/ Return the start and end page frames for a node @nid\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} nid to return the range for. If M\+A\+X\+\_\+\+N\+U\+M\+N\+O\+D\+ES, the min and max P\+FN are returned. @start\+\_\+pfn\+: Passed by reference. On return, it will have the node start\+\_\+pfn. @end\+\_\+pfn\+: Passed by reference. On return, it will have the node end\+\_\+pfn.

It returns the start and end page frame of a node based on information provided by \mbox{\hyperlink{memblock_8c_ad0e685a82a3772b25b78e30e34fa8595}{memblock\+\_\+set\+\_\+node()}}. If called for a node with no available memory, a warning is printed and the start and end P\+F\+Ns will be 0. Here is the caller graph for this function\+:

\index{page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}!node\_map\_pfn\_alignment@{node\_map\_pfn\_alignment}}
\index{node\_map\_pfn\_alignment@{node\_map\_pfn\_alignment}!page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{node\_map\_pfn\_alignment()}{node\_map\_pfn\_alignment()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily unsigned long \mbox{\hyperlink{test__stackinit_8c_aca8f1e05ca62886e6d99e8dc006e5500}{\+\_\+\+\_\+init}} node\+\_\+map\+\_\+pfn\+\_\+alignment (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void}]{ }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

node\+\_\+map\+\_\+pfn\+\_\+alignment -\/ determine the maximum internode alignment

This function should be called after node map is populated and sorted. It calculates the maximum power of two alignment which can distinguish all the nodes.

For example, if all nodes are 1GiB and aligned to 1GiB, the return value would indicate 1GiB alignment with (1 $<$$<$ (30 -\/ P\+A\+G\+E\+\_\+\+S\+H\+I\+FT)). If the nodes are shifted by 256MiB, 256MiB. Note that if only the last node is shifted, 1GiB is enough and this function will indicate so.

This is used to test whether pfn -\/$>$ nid mapping of the chosen memory model has fine enough granularity to avoid incorrect mapping for the populated node map.

Return\+: the determined alignment in pfn\textquotesingle{}s. 0 if there is no alignment requirement (single node). Here is the call graph for this function\+:

nr\+\_\+free\+\_\+zone\+\_\+pages -\/ count number of pages beyond high watermark @offset\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} zone index of the highest zone

\mbox{\hyperlink{page__alloc_8c_acb7b2482fa1722eb8324534aa8a0372e}{nr\+\_\+free\+\_\+zone\+\_\+pages()}} counts the number of pages which are beyond the high watermark within all zones at or below a given zone index. For each zone, the number of pages is calculated as\+: \begin{DoxyVerb}nr_free_zone_pages = managed_pages - high_pages
\end{DoxyVerb}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{set\_dma\_reserve()}{set\_dma\_reserve()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \mbox{\hyperlink{test__stackinit_8c_aca8f1e05ca62886e6d99e8dc006e5500}{\+\_\+\+\_\+init}} set\+\_\+dma\+\_\+reserve (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned long}]{new\+\_\+dma\+\_\+reserve }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

set\+\_\+dma\+\_\+reserve -\/ set the specified number of pages reserved in the first zone @new\+\_\+dma\+\_\+reserve\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} number of pages to mark reserved

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} per-\/cpu batchsize and zone watermarks are determined by managed\+\_\+pages. In the D\+MA zone, a significant percentage may be consumed by kernel image and other unfreeable allocations which can skew the watermarks badly. This function may optionally be used to account for unfreeable pages in the first zone (e.\+g., Z\+O\+N\+E\+\_\+\+D\+MA). \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} effect will be lower watermarks and smaller per-\/cpu batchsize.
